ParlaMint-PL_2017-12-06-sejm-53-1.u605	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Members of the applicant, I have the honour to present a draft law amending the Law on the Specific Rules for the Removal of Legal Effects of Reprivatization Decisions concerning Warsaw Real Estates, issued in violation of the law and certain other laws. The Law of 9 March 2017 on specific rules for the removal of legal effects of reprivatization decisions constitutes the legal basis for the functioning of the Commission for the disposal of legal effects of reprivatization decisions concerning Warsaw real estate, issued in breach of the law. A detailed examination of the provisions of the Act leads to the conclusion that it is justified to introduce legislative amendments which will comprehensively counter the length of proceedings pending before the Commission and increase citizens' confidence in the committees and their decisions. The recognition of administrative matters in many cases is characterised by chronicity. This problem is particularly important in relation to difficult proceedings related to the re-privatization decisions concerning Warsaw real estate. The obligation laid down in the Act to challenge the decision of a committee by administrative procedure before bringing an action before an administrative court, which in turn controls the content of the decision and can in principle only repeal the contested decision, leaving the substantive decision of the committee, is a purposeful assumption. Importantly, in the case of decisions taken at first instance by the Commission, the party wishing to decide on the review of the administrative court's case must submit a request for review by the same authority, although the effectiveness of such requests would be very limited. The proceedings before the committee are closely related to many areas of life and concern the everyday affairs of all citizens. The speed of handling cases and the quality of decisions have a significant and direct impact on the assessment of the whole public administration system and the level of confidence of individuals in the state. The analysis of the dysfunctions of the procedures provided for in the Act therefore has a universal impact, which makes it necessary for the solutions that seek to limit them to be decisive. The aim of the project is to ensure the smooth functioning of the committee. The project, which I have the honour to present, also aims to ensure the mobilisation of fines in cases of infringement. This year it has been 97 years since the most important victory of Poland over the invader in 1920, and we still owe those heroes. After all, we have committed ourselves to placing a triumphal bow in their honor. It is difficult to imagine in the capital of our country two monuments at the same time: one in honor of our great ancestors, the other in honor of our invaders. To quote Marek Rymkiewicz: "We exist - and we realize that we exist - only because we remember."

At the last meeting of the Sejm, the governing majority addressed to further work a law introducing compulsory ZUS contributions on salaries higher than 30 times the average salary per year, i.e. it simply deleted the limit that is currently in force, because now, after exceeding the annual income above 30 times the average salary - this year, it is about 127 thousand. gross - social security contributions are no longer collected. Income of 127 thousand. gives a salary of approx. PLN 6700 per month per hand. Law and Justice, introducing mandatory ZUS contributions from each salary, i.e. also from the higher ones, freeing this limit, only cares for a temporary increase in revenue to the budget, because you calculated that next year it would affect the budget of about PLN 5.4 billion. This is a lack of responsibility for future generations. Our children will have to pay for your wrong decisions. And what, for example? With the father of four kids with a wife who wasn't working? In order to support his family, he often has to earn two jobs, earning more than 30 times his income. Such a person will have to pay a much larger ZUS, and therefore will have a much lower salary. It is the responsibility of every responsible government in every country to think about future generations, not just the end of its own nose, that is, next election year. The entry into force of this law will result in a huge gap in the amount of pensions paid in the future. Can we afford to pay our pension, for example? for the president of a foreign bank of approx. 40 thousand. Evil? This is indecent, especially when we consider that most pensions in a dozen years will oscillate, it will amount to about PLN 1000.1500 per month. Avalanches of actions may also be brought to the courts of pensioners who paid high contributions before 1999 and pensions are as limited as those who then paid small contributions. Even if this law enters into force with a deferred deadline, e.g. there will be a long vacatio legis, in a year's time, these legal pensioners can claim their rights after the law has been passed. Is the ZUS budget prepared for this? Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-12-06-sejm-53-1.u642	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Tonight at the hour. 10 in the church of the Savior began the prayer of the Rosary, and later, at 3 p.m. Eleven, Holy Mass took place. Funeral in behalf of the deceased Fr. Prelate Konrad Herrmann. After her, the coffin with the priest's body was transported to the Old Cemetery on the street. Wroclaw in Zielona Góra. The Great Honourable Priest Conrad! This letter, by its very nature farewell, is basically another conversation with you about important, fundamental, unfailing matters, regardless of our physical presence in any of the worlds. My God is the God of love - you have spoken in many conversations, including those whose way to the Most High ran by winding paths, sometimes it is still being made or waiting to be discovered, because your home - as well as the heart and time that you always seemed to have for others - was constantly open and hospitable to anyone who would just ascend to it. If I were to categorize your attitude, she was soaked in God's Mercy. You didn't ask what hump of sinfulness, self-righteousness or wandering along the paths of life someone came to you. You were mostly interested in how, through the support of God's providence and the Holy Spirit, you could be helpful to man wherever he went. No wonder, then, that you were always surrounded by thirsty counsel, closeness, wisdom, and above all spiritual sensitivity of the crowd. You never left him behind in the sense of abandoning or ignoring him. On the contrary, he was always first, Jesus, and immediately after him a priestly servant man was given. And every one of the green mountains knew it, for he felt it, because the goodness and warmth of you, Father Conrad, emanated distinctly. What would you say to God if you stood before him? - You were asked years ago by a journalist from Radio West. Mercy I would ask - you answered. Although I have served him my whole life, many things I would do differently now, better - you added. Today, your words become a task for us, and especially for those who zealously refer to your biography and enter into a special confidence with you. You were expressive in your enlightened humanism, your high personal culture, your great humility towards the undisputed. At the same time, you were characterized by the ability not only charismatic, but also the perfect for years of easing tensions, eliminating disputes and crystallizing good. The most common gesture, the intention you entrusted to people, was the gesture given to the hand, while not rejecting the one that is drawn in your direction. Although the daily life and the time of your priestly ministry took place in times of significant tensions and disputes, including birth, the festival of solidarity, there was no anger and fierceness towards anyone. On the contrary, as you understood the disastrous experience of the Polish-Jaruzel war, which affected both sides of the barricades, you were going to use mercy as the most desirable weapon. The church of Zielona Góra and Gorzów is saying goodbye to you. He will be able to walk you to a place of eternal rest with dignity and beauty. Faithful, who with such zeal and warmth embraced your prayer, pastoral commitment in various areas of life, daily, humanly regret that man's inexhaustible biological nature takes you away from us. Nevertheless, in our Christian consciousness, we are convinced that the way of your noble life has ended only here, and that through your many talents and charisms you have been forced by your deserved presence and proximity in the Father's house. In view of these facts, we cannot do anything other than to remain faithful to your signs, and this direction shows us the need to remain faithful to God, to love our homeland and to serve our fellowman. Give him eternal rest, Lord.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-12-06-sejm-53-1.u646	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! In November, an away meeting of the Parliamentary Team on relations with the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia took place in Rzeszow. The aim of the team was to show the attractiveness of business relationships that our companies can establish with Saudi partners. Will this procedure have the desired effect? The presence of Ewa Leniart - Podkarpackie Voivodeship, Piotr Pilcha - Vice-voivodeship, Władysław Ortyla - Marshal of Podkarpackie Voivodeship, Maria Kurowska - Vice-Marshal of the Voivodeship and Józef Jodłowski - Starosta of the Rzeszowskie district testify to the high rank of the meeting of the Parliamentary Team for relations with the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia. Parliament represented: Krystyna Wróblewska - Chair of the Contact Team with the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia, Piotr Uruski, Bogdan Rzońc and Mieczysław Miazga. As the members of the parliamentary team claim, he was selected for the meeting of the Rzeszów on purpose. Saudis are interested in innovative technologies, cooperation with aviation and IT companies. In our voivodship there are many such companies, the core of which are companies forming part of the corporations of global aviation giants. Next to them there are smaller Polish companies that need new business partners. We invited the authorities of the Polish-Saudian Business Council, which has been associating large Polish companies with partners from Saudi Arabia for years. We also hosted Turkish Al-Angari, a representative of Saudi entrepreneurs from the IT and aviation industry. Marek Gawlik, President of the Polish-Saudian Business Council, believes that Saudi entrepreneurs are among the very difficult and demanding business partners. This comes from a different, incomprehensible culture for Poles, as well as from other mechanisms of functioning in the business environments in Europe. We must remember that Saudi Arabia is a powerful country. Its gross domestic product represents the fourth part of the GDP of all Arab countries. It is a country in which powerful infrastructure, housing and industrial investments are being created in front of our eyes. It's a country that imports 98% consumed food. Members of the Parliamentary Contact Team with the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia are convinced that the meeting held in Rzeszow will have tangible effects, will be interested in local forms, will encourage entry into the Saudi market, but will also attract capital from this country to Poland. Podkarpackie region is a very interesting place in Poland. Before I came here, I heard a lot about investment opportunities. Especially interesting are the special economic zones where you can locate many investments - Turki Al Angari, who introduced American giants like the Cisco System to the Saudi market. He assures us that after his return to the country he will hold a number of meetings with Saudi entrepreneurs. It is very likely that it is in Podkarpackie Voivodship that the surplus of their capital will begin to be invested. Interests of investors are not covered by the authorities of the district of Rzeszów, which supervise the functioning of the large Special Economic Zone Rzeszów-Dworszko. We care about investors. Every new factory is a place of work for the residents of our county, it is to increase the attractiveness of the county and the quality of life. We are open to investors and we will provide all the help that they will appear - says Józef Jodłowski, the starost of the district of Rzeszów. Saudi Arabia is a big country. Its area is several times larger than that of Poland. However, the population is a few million less than in Poland, but according to many sources, this number exceeds 30 million. Importing food for such a large population entails the need to carry massive investments, and this represents a huge opportunity for Polish companies. The construction of the city on the coast of the Red Sea is also a great opportunity for Polish entrepreneurs. It's the idea of Prince Muhammad ibn Salman. Saudi Arabia's authorities are slowly preparing for a time when oil deposits will run out. The first phase of construction is to be completed by 2025. However, in order to show the scale of this construction, let me just say that this city is going to be more than 30 times bigger than New York - says Piotr Gierałtowski, Vice-President of the Polish-Saudian Business Council. It also notes that such a powerful investment concept is also a challenge and opportunity for Polish designers and construction companies specialising in interior finishing. Thank you very much.
